           {0, 11, 5}, {0, 5, 1}, {0, 1, 7}, {0, 7, 10}, {0, 10, 11},\n            {1, 5, 9}, {5, 11, 4}, {11, 10, 2}, {10, 7, 6}, {7, 1, 8},\n            {3, 9, 4}, {3, 4, 2}, {3, 2, 6}, {3, 6, 8}, std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> newFaces;\n            std::map<std::pair<idx_t, idx_t>, idx_t> midpointCache;\n\n            auto getMidpoint = [&](idx_t i1, idx_t i2) -> idx_t {\n                auto key = std::make_pair(std::min(i1, i2), std::max(i1, i2));\n                auto it = midpointCache.find(key);\n                if (it != midpointCache.end()) return it->second;\n\n // Reverse winding to make normals point inward\n            triangles.emplace_back(vertices[face[2]], vertices[face[1]], vertices[face[0]]);\n        }\n    }\n};\n\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Check if a ray between two triangles is blocked by any other triangle\n// Uses octree for O(log N) instead of O(N) srcTriIdx || idx == dstTriIdx) return false;\n\n        val_t dist = rayTriangleIntersect(from, dirNorm, tri.a, tri.b, tri.c);\n        if (dist > EPSILON && dist < rayLen - EPSILON) {\n ============================================================================\n// Precomputation Phase\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"Computing form factors (Kij)...\\n\");\n    RandomGenerator rng(42);\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {\n            if (i == j) continue;\n            state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeKij(\n                i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);\n        }\n        if ((i + 1) % 100 == 0 || i + 1 == state.numTriangles) {\n            printf(\"  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\\n\", i + 1, state.numTriangles);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"Computing time delays (Tau)...\\n\");\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {\n            if (i == j) continue;\n            state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeTau(\n                state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j]);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"Running wave propagation simulation...\\n\");\n\n    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n            val_t sumB = ZERO;\n\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {\n                if (i == j) continue;\n\n                int tauij = state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)];\n\n                // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i\n                if (static_cast<int>(t) < tauij) continue;\n\n                val_t kij = state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)];\n                if (kij <= ZERO) continue;\n\n                // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred\n                size_t srcTime = t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);\n                val_t radJ = state.radB[state.idxTN(srcTime, j)];\n                if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;\n\n                // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity\n                sumB += std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE) * radJ;\n            }\n\n            // Update radiosity: reflection + emission\n            state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] = state.rho[i] * sumB + state.radE[state.idxTN(t, i)];\n        }\n\n        if ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps) {\n            printf(\"  Timestep %zu/%zu\\n\", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"Computing distances via cross-correlation...\\n\");\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;\n        int bestT = 0;\n\n        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n            val_t sum = ZERO;\n\n            for (size_t tt = t; tt < state.numTimesteps; ++tt) {\n                val_t pB = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt, i)];\n                val_t pS = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt - t, state.sourceIndex)];\n                sum += pS * pB;\n            }\n\n            if (sum > maxCorr) {\n                maxCorr = sum;\n                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);\n            }\n        }\n\n        state.distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Validation\n// ============================================================================\n\nbool validateResults(const SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"\\nValidation:\\n\");\n\n    // Check that distances are non-negative\n    bool allNonNegative = true;\n    val_t minDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    val_t maxDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t sumDist = ZERO;\n    int nonZeroCount = 0;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        val_t d = state.distances[i];\n        if (d < 0) {\n            allNonNegative = false;\n            printf(\"  ERROR: Negative distance at triangle %zu: %f\\n\", i, d);\n        }\n        if (!std::isfinite(d)) {\n            printf(\"  ERROR: Non-finite distance at triangle %zu: %f\\n\", i, d);\n            return false;\n        }\n        minDist = std::min(minDist, d);\n        maxDist = std::max(maxDist, d);\n        sumDist += d;\n        if (d > EPSILON) nonZeroCount++;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Distance range: [%.4f, %.4f]\\n\", minDist, maxDist);\n    printf(\"  Average distance: %.4f\\n\", sumDist / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles));\n    printf(\"  Non-zero distances: %d/%zu\\n\", nonZeroCount, state.numTriangles);\n\n    // Check source distance is zero or very small\n    val_t srcDist = state.distances[state.sourceIndex];\n    if (srcDist > WAVE_SPEED * 2) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Source triangle distance is non-zero: %.4f\\n\", srcDist);\n    }\n\n    // Check radiosity propagation (some triangles should have received energy)\n    int receivedEnergy = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n            if (state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] > EPSILON) {\n                receivedEnergy++;\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Triangles receiving energy: %d/%zu\\n\", receivedEnergy, state.numTriangles);\n\n    if (receivedEnergy == 0) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: No triangles received energy - simulation failed\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check Kij matrix (should have some non-zero entries)\n    int nonZeroKij = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        if (state.kij[i] > EPSILON) nonZeroKij++;\n    }\n    printf(\"  Non-zero form factors: %d/%zu (%.2f%%)\\n\",\n           nonZeroKij, state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles,\n           100.0f * nonZeroKij / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles));\n\n    if (nonZeroKij == 0) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: All form factors are zero - visibility computation failed\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (!allNonNegative) {\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Hash for Verification\n// ============================================================================\n\nuint64_t computeHash(const SimulationState& state) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        const uint32_t* ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint32_t*>(&state.distances[i]);\n        hash ^= (static_cast<uint64_t>(*ptr) + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\n// intersects this node's bounding box\\n    bool rayIntersectsBox(const Vec3& p1, const Vec3& p2) const {\\n        Vec3 d = (p2 - p1) * 0.5f;\\n        Vec3 c = p1 + d - center;\\n        Vec3 ad = {std::abs(d.x), std::abs(d.y), std::abs(d.z)};\\n\\n        if (std::abs(c.x) > halfExtent.x + ad.x) return false;\\n        if (std::abs(c.y) > halfExtent.y + ad.y) return false;\\n        if (std::abs(c.z) > halfExtent.z + ad.z) return false;\\n\\n        if (std::abs(d.y * c.z - d.z * c.y) > halfExtent.y * ad.z + halfExtent.z * ad.y + EPSILON) return false;\\n        if (std::abs(d.z * c.x - d.x * c.z) > halfExtent.z * ad.x + halfExtent.x * ad.z + EPSILON) return false;\\n        if (std::abs(d.x * c.y - d.y * c.x) > halfExtent.x * ad.y + halfExtent.y * ad.x + EPSILON) return false;\\n\\n        return true;\\n    }\\n\\n    // Apply a function to all triangles potentially intersecting the ray\\n    // Returns true if the function returns true for any triangle\\n    template<typename Func>\\n    bool applyToTris(const Vec3& p1, const Vec3& p2, Func&& func) const {\\n        // If leaf node, check triangles directly\\n        if (!triangleIndices.empty()) {\\n            for (size_t idx : triangleIndices) {\\n                if (func(idx, (*allTriangles)[idx])) return true;\\n            }\\n            return false;\\n        }\\n\\n        // Otherwise, descend to children\\n        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {\\n            if (children[i] && children[i]->rayIntersectsBox(p1, p2)) {\\n                if (children[i]->applyToTris(p1, p2, func)) ============================================================================\\n\\n// Generate an icosphere by subdividing an icosahedron\\nclass IcosphereMesh {\\npublic:\\n    std::vector<Triangle> vertices = {\\n            Vec3(-1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,\\n            Vec3( 1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,\\n            Vec3(-1, -t,  0).normalized() * radius,\\n            Vec3( 1,       Vec3( 0,  1, -t).normalized() * radius,\\n            Vec3( t,  0, -1).normalized() * radius,\\n            Vec3( t,  0,  1).normalized() * radius,\\n            Vec3(-t,  0, -1).normalized() * radius,\\n            Vec3(-t,  0,  1).normalized() * radius\\n        };\\n\\n        // Initial icosahedron faces (20 triangles)\\n        std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> faces = {\\n            {0, 11, 5}, {0, 5, 1}, {0, 1, 7}, {0, 7, 10}, {0, 10, 11},\\n            {1, 5, 9}, {5, 11, 4}, {11, 10, 2}, {10, 7, 6}, {7, 1, 8},\\n            {3, 9, 4}, {3, 4, 2}, {3, 2, 6}, {3, 6, 8}, {3, 8, 9},\\n            {4, 9, 5}, {2, 4, 11}, {6, 2, 10}, {8, 6, 7}, {9, 8, 1}\\n        };\\n\\n        // Subdivide\\n        for (int i = 0; i < subdivisions; ++i) {\\n            std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> newFaces;\\n            std::map<std::pair<idx_t, idx_t>, idx_t> midpointCache;\\n\\n            auto getMidpoint = [&](idx_t i1, idx_t i2) -> idx_t {\\n                auto key = std::make_pair(std::min(i1, i2), std::max(i1, i2));\\n                auto it = midpointCache.find(key);\\n                if (it != midpointCache.end()) return it->second;\\n\\n                Vec3 mid = (vertices[i1] + vertices[i2]) / 2.0f;\\n                mid = mid.normalized() * radius;\\n                idx_t idx = static_cast<idx_t>(vertices.size());\\n                vertices.push_back(mid);\\n                midpointCache[key] = idx;\\n                return idx;\\n            };\\n\\n            for (const auto& face : faces) {\\n                idx_t a = getMidpoint(face[0], face[1]);\\n                idx_t b = getMidpoint(face[1], face[2]);\\n                idx_t c = getMidpoint(face[2], face[0]);\\n\\n                newFaces.push_back({face[0], a, c});\\n                newFaces.push_back({face[1], b, a});\\n                newFaces.push_back({face[2], c, b});\\n                newFaces.push_back({a, b, c});\\n            }\\n            faces = std::move(newFaces);\\n        }\\n\\n        // Build triangles (normals pointing inward for a \\\"room\\\")\\n        triangles.reserve(faces.size());\\n        for (const auto& face : faces) {\\n            // Reverse winding to make normals point inward\\n            triangles.emplace_back(vertices[face[2]], vertices[face[1]], vertices[face[0]]);\\n        }\\n    }\\n};\\n\\n// RandomGenerator {\\n    std::mt19937 rng;\\n    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;\\npublic:\\n    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}\\n    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }\\n};\\n\\n// Generate a random point inside a triangle using barycentric coordinates\\nVec3 randomPointInTriangle(const Triangle& t, RandomGenerator& rng) {\\n    val_t u = rng.rand();\\n    val_t v = rng.rand();\\n    if (u + v > 1.0f) {\\n        u = 1.0f - u;\\n        v = 1.0f - v;\\n    }\\n    Vec3 ab = t.b - t.a;\\n    Vec3 ac = t.c - t.a;\\n    return t.a + ab * u + ac * v;\\n}\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Ray-Triangle Intersection (Möller-Trumbore algorithm)\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\nval_t rayTriangleIntersect(const Vec3& orig, const Vec3& dir,\\n                            const Vec3& v0, const Vec3& v1, const Vec3& v2) {\\n    Vec3 e1 = v1 - v0;\\n    Vec3 e2 = v2 - v0;\\n    Vec3 pvec = dir.cross(e2);\\n    val_t det = e1.dot(pvec);\\n\\n    if (std::abs(det) < EPSILON) return == srcTriIdx || idx == dstTriIdx) return false;\\n\\n        val_t dist = rayTriangleIntersect(from, dirNorm, tri.a, tri.b, tri.c);\\n        if (dist > EPSILON && dist < rayLen - EPSILON) {\\n            return true;  // Ray is blocked\\n        }\\n        return false;\\n    });\\n}\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Form Factor Computation (Kij)\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\n// Compute the cosine of angle between vector and triangle normal\\nval_t cosPhi(const Vec3& v, const Vec3& normal) {\\n    val_t vNorm = v.norm();\\n    if (vNorm <= EPSILON) return ZERO;\\n    return std::max(ZERO, v.dot(normal) / vNorm);\\n}\\n\\n// Compute form factor Kij between triangle i (receiver) and triangle j (emitter)\\nval_t computeKij(size_t idxI, size_t idxJ,\\n                  const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles,\\n                  const Octree& octree,\\n                  RandomGenerator& rng) {\\n    const Triangle& triI = triangles[idxI];\\n    const Triangle& triJ = triangles[idxJ];\\n\\n    // Cull triangles facing the same direction\\n    if (triI.normal().dot(triJ.normal()) > 0.99f) return ZERO;\\n\\n    val_t kij = ZERO;\\n\\n    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {\\n        Vec3 pI = randomPointInTriangle(triI, rng);\\n        Vec3 pJ w >= 0; --w) {\n                    if (!src[w]) continue;\n                    dst[w + ws] ^= src[w] << bs;\n                    if (bs && w + ws + 1 < W) dst[w + ws + 1] ^= src[w] >> (64 - bs);\n                }\n            };\n            for (int n = 0; k >= MEXP; --k) {\n                if (!((a[k >> 6] >> (k & 63)) & 1u)) continue;\n                int sh = k - MEXP;\n                int ws = sh >> 6;\n                const Poly& ps = psh[sh & 63];\n                for (int w = 0; w < SW && w + ws < static_cast<int>(a.size()); ++w) a[w + ws] ^= ps[w];\n            }\n        };\n        auto spread = [](uint32_t v) -> uint64_t {\n            uint64_t z = v;\n            z = (z | (z << 16)) & 0x0000FFFF0000FFFFull;\n            z = (z | (z << 8)) & 0x00FF00FF00FF00FFull;\n            z = (z | (z << 4)) & 0x0F0F0F0F0F0F0F0Full;\n            z = (z | (z << 2)) & 0x3333333333333333ull;\n            z = (z | (z << 1)) & 0x5555555555555555ull;\n            return z;\n        };\n        int top = 63 - __builtin_clzll(n);\n        for (int b = top; b >= 0; --b) {\n            // square\n            std::fill(sq.begin(), sq.end(), 0);\n            for (int w = 0; w < PW; ++w) {\n                sq[2 * w] = spread(static_cast<uint32_t>(r[w]));\n                sq[2 * w + 1] = spread(static_cast<uint32_t>(r[w] >> 32));\n            }\n            reduce(sq, 2 * MEXP - 2);\n            for (int w = 0; w < PW; ++w) r[w] = sq[w];\n            // multiply by x\n            if ((n >> b) & 1u) {\n                std::fill(sq.begin(), sq.end(), 0);\n                for (int w = 0; w < PW; ++w) {\n                    sq[w] |= r[w] << 1;\n                    sq[w + 1] |= r[w] >> 63;\n                }\n                reduce(sq, MEXP);\n                for (int w = 0; w < PW; ++w) r[w] = sq[w];\n            }\n        }\n        return r;\n    }\n};\n\nclass RandomGenerator {\n    MT19937 rng;\n    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;\npublic:\n    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}\n    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }\n    void skip(uint64_t numDraws) { rng.jump(numDraws); }\n};","originalFile":"/**\n * Room Response Simulation Benchmark\n * \n * This is a simplified, purely sequential implementation of room impulse response\n * simulation using radiosity-based wave propagation. ============================================================================\n// Triangle-Box Overlap Test (for octree construction)\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Separating axis test for triangle-box overlap\nbool triangleBoxOverlap(const Vec3& boxCenter, const Vec3& boxHalfSize, const Triangle& tri) {\n    // Translate triangle to box center\n    Vec3 v0 = tri.a - boxCenter;\n    Vec3 v1 = tri.b - boxCenter;\n    Vec3 v2 = tri.c - boxCenter;\n\n    // Triangle std::make_pair(std::min({a, b, c}), std::max({a, b, c}));\n    };\n\n    auto [minX, maxX] = minMax3(v0.x, v1.x, v2.x);\n    if (minX > boxHalfSize.x || maxX < -boxHalfSize.x) return false;\n\n    auto [minY, maxY] = minMax3(v0.y, v1.y, v2.y);\n    if (minY > boxHalfSize.y || maxY < -boxHalfSize.y) return false;\n\n    auto [minZ, maxZ] = minMax3(v0.z, v1.z, v2.z);\n    if (minZ > boxHalfSize.z || maxZ < -boxHalfSize.z) return false;\n\n    // Test triangle normal\n    Vec3 triNormal = e0.cross(e1);\n    val_t d = triNormal.dot(v0);\n    val_t r = boxHalfSize.x * std::abs(triNormal.x) +\n              boxHalfSize.y * std::abs(triNormal.y) +\n              boxHalfSize.z * std::abs(triNormal.z);\n    if (std::abs(d) > r) return false;\n\n    // Test 9 edge cross products\n    auto testAxis = [&](const Vec3& axis) {\n        val_t p0 = axis.dot(v0);\n        val_t p1 = axis.dot(v1);\n        val_t p2 = axis.dot(v2);\n        val_t r = boxHalfSize.x * std::abs(axis.x) +\n                  boxHalfSize.y * std::abs(axis.y) +\n                  boxHalfSize.z * std::abs(axis.z);\n        auto [minP, maxP] = minMax3(p0, p1, p2);\n        return !(minP > r || maxP < -r);\n    };\n\n    Vec3 ============================================================================\n\nconstexpr size_t MAX_OCTREE_TRIS = 8;\nconstexpr val_t MAX_OCTREE_LEAF_SIZE = 0.5f;\n\nclass Octree {\npublic:\n    Vec3      minBound = nodeMin;\n        maxBound = nodeMax;\n        halfExtent = (maxBound - minBound) * 0.5f;\n        center = (minBound + maxBound) * 0.5f;\n\n        // If few enough triangles or too small, make this a leaf\n        if (indices.size() <= MAX_OCTREE_TRIS ||\n            (maxBound - minBound).norm() < MAX_OCTREE_LEAF_SIZE) {\n            triangleIndices = indices;\n                childCenter.x += (i & 1) ? childHalfSize.x : -childHalfSize.x;\n                childCenter.y += (i & 2) ? childHalfSize.y : -childHalfSize.y;\n                childCenter.z += (i & 4) }\n        }\n\n        // Check if we can't split further (all triangles in one child)\n        bool canSplit = false;\n        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {\n            if (!childIndices[i].empty() && childIndices[i].size() < indices.size()) {\n                canSplit = true;\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (!canSplit) {\n            triangleIndices = indices;\n Vec3 childMax = center;\n                childMin.x = (i & 1) ? center.x : minBound.x;\n                childMax.x = (i & 1) ? maxBound.x : center.x;\n                childMin.y = (i & 2) ? center.y : minBound.y;\n                childMax.y = (i & 2) ? maxBound.y : center.y;\n                childMin.z = (i & 4) ? center.z : minBound.z;\n                childMax.z = (i & 4) ? maxBound.z : (p2 - p1) * 0.5f;\n        Vec3 c = p1 + d - center;\n        Vec3 ad = {std::abs(d.x), std::abs(d.y), std::abs(d.z)};\n\n        if (std::abs(c.x) > halfExtent.x + ad.x) return false;\n        if (std::abs(c.y) > halfExtent.y + ad.y) return false;\n        if (std::abs(c.z) > halfExtent.z + ad.z) return false;\n\n        if (std::abs(d.y * c.z - d.z * c.y) > halfExtent.y * ad.z + halfExtent.z * ad.y + EPSILON) return false;\n        if (std::abs(d.z * c.x - d.x * c.z) > halfExtent.z * ad.x + halfExtent.x * ad.z + EPSILON) return false;\n        if (std::abs(d.x * c.y - d.y * c.x) > halfExtent.x * ad.y + halfExtent.y * ad.x + EPSILON) return false;\n\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    // Apply a function to all triangles potentially intersecting the ray\n    // Returns true if the function returns true for any triangle\n    template<typename Func>\n    bool applyToTris(const Vec3& p1, const Vec3& p2, Func&& func) const {\n        // If leaf node, check triangles directly\n        if (!triangleIndices.empty()) {\n            for (size_t idx : triangleIndices) {\n                if (func(idx, (*allTriangles)[idx])) return true;\n            }\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        // Otherwise, descend to children\n        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {\n            if (children[i] && children[i]->rayIntersectsBox(p1, p2)) {\n                if (children[i]->applyToTris(p1, p2, func)) return true;\n            }\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n};\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Mesh Generation: Icosphere\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Generate an icosphere by subdividing an icosahedron\nclass IcosphereMesh {\npublic:\n    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\n\n    IcosphereMesh(int subdivisions, val_t radius) {\n        // Initial icosahedron vertices\n        const val_t t = (1.0f + std::sqrt(5.0f)) / 2.0f;\n\n        std::vector<Vec3> vertices = {\n            Vec3(-1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3(-1, -t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 1, -t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 0, -1,  t).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 0,  1,  t).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 0, -1, -t).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 0,  1, -t).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( t,  0, -1).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( t,  0,  1).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3(-t,  0, -1).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3(-t,  0,  1).normalized() * radius\n        };\n\n        // Initial icosahedron faces (20 triangles)\n        std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> faces = {\n            {0, 11, 5}, {0, 5, 1}, {0, 1, 7}, {0, 7, 10}, {0, 10, 11},\n            {1, 5, 9}, {5, 11, 4}, {11, 10, 2}, {10, 7, 6}, {7, 1, 8},\n            {3, 9, 4}, {3, 4, 2}, {3, 2, 6}, {3, 6, 8}, {3, 8, 9},\n            {4, 9, 5}, {2, 4, 11}, {6, 2, 10}, {8, 6, 7}, {9, 8, 1}\n        };\n\n        // Subdivide\n        for (int i = 0; i < subdivisions; ++i) {\n            std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> newFaces;\n            std::map<std::pair<idx_t, idx_t>, idx_t> midpointCache;\n\n            auto getMidpoint = [&](idx_t i1, idx_t i2) -> idx_t {\n                auto key = std::make_pair(std::min(i1, i2), std::max(i1, i2));\n                auto it = midpointCache.find(key);\n                if (it != midpointCache.end()) return it->second;\n\n                Vec3 mid = (vertices[i1] + vertices[i2]) / 2.0f;\n                mid = mid.normalized() * radius;\n                idx_t idx = static_cast<idx_t>(vertices.size());\n                vertices.push_back(mid);\n                midpointCache[key] = idx;\n                return idx;\n            };\n\n            for (const auto& face : faces) {\n                idx_t a = getMidpoint(face[0], face[1]);\n                idx_t b = getMidpoint(face[1], face[2]);\n                idx_t c = getMidpoint(face[2], face[0]);\n\n                newFaces.push_back({face[0], a, c});\n                newFaces.push_back({face[1], b, a});\n                newFaces.push_back({face[2], c, b});\n                newFaces.push_back({a, b, c});\n            }\n            faces = std::move(newFaces);\n        }\n\n        // Build triangles (normals pointing inward for a \"room\")\n        triangles.reserve(faces.size());\n        for (const auto& face : faces) {\n            // Reverse winding to make normals point inward\n            triangles.emplace_back(vertices[face[2]], vertices[face[1]], vertices[face[0]]);\n        }\n    }\n};\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Random Number Generation\n// ============================================================================\n\nclass RandomGenerator {\n    std::mt19937 rng;\n    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;\npublic:\n    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}\n    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }\n};\n\n// Generate a random point inside a triangle using barycentric coordinates\nVec3 randomPointInTriangle(const Triangle& t, RandomGenerator& rng) {\n    val_t u = rng.rand();\n    val_t v = rng.rand();\n    if (u + v > 1.0f) {\n        u = 1.0f - u;\n        v = 1.0f - v;\n    }\n    Vec3 ab = t.b - t.a;\n    Vec3 ac = t.c - t.a;\n    return t.a + ab * u + ac * v;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Ray-Triangle Intersection (Möller-Trumbore algorithm)\n// ============================================================================\n\nval_t rayTriangleIntersect(const Vec3& orig, const Vec3& dir,\n                            const Vec3& v0, const Vec3& v1, const Vec3& v2) {\n    Vec3 e1 = v1 - v0;\n    Vec3 e2 = v2 - v0;\n    Vec3 pvec = dir.cross(e2);\n    val_t det = e1.dot(pvec);\n\n    if (std::abs(det) < EPSILON) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    val_t invDet = 1.0f / det;\n    Vec3 tvec = orig - v0;\n    val_t u = tvec.dot(pvec) * invDet;\n    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    Vec3 qvec = tvec.cross(e1);\n    val_t v = dir.dot(qvec) * invDet;\n    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    return e2.dot(qvec) * invDet;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Visibility Testing (Octree-accelerated)\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Check if a ray between two triangles is blocked by any other triangle\n// Uses octree for O(log N) instead of O(N) search\nbool isRayBlocked(const Vec3& from, const Vec3& to,\n                  const Octree& octree,\n                  size_t srcTriIdx, size_t dstTriIdx) {\n    Vec3 dir = to - from;\n    val_t rayLen = dir.norm();\n    if (rayLen < EPSILON) return true;\n    Vec3 dirNorm = dir / rayLen;\n\n    return octree.applyToTris(from, to, [&](size_t idx, const Triangle& tri) {\n        if (idx == srcTriIdx || idx == dstTriIdx) return false;\n\n        val_t dist = rayTriangleIntersect(from, dirNorm, tri.a, tri.b, tri.c);\n        if (dist > EPSILON && dist < rayLen - EPSILON) {\n            return true;  // Ray is blocked\n        }\n        return false;\n    });\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Form Factor Computation (Kij)\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Compute the cosine of angle between vector and triangle normal\nval_t cosPhi(const Vec3& v, const Vec3& normal) {\n    val_t vNorm = v.norm();\n    if (vNorm <= EPSILON) return ZERO;\n    return std::max(ZERO, v.dot(normal) / vNorm);\n}\n\n// Compute form factor Kij between triangle i (receiver) and triangle j (emitter)\nval_t computeKij(size_t idxI, size_t idxJ,\n                  const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles,\n                  const Octree& octree,\n                  RandomGenerator& rng) {\n    const Triangle& triI = triangles[idxI];\n    const Triangle& triJ = triangles[idxJ];\n\n    // Cull triangles facing the same direction\n    if (triI.normal().dot(triJ.normal()) > 0.99f) return ZERO;\n\n    val_t kij = ZERO;\n\n    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {\n        Vec3 pI = randomPointInTriangle(triI, rng);\n        Vec3 pJ = randomPointInTriangle(triJ, rng);\n\n        if (isRayBlocked(pI, pJ, octree, idxI, idxJ)) continue;\n\n        Vec3 v = pJ - pI;\n        val_t distSqr = v.squaredNorm();\n        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;\n\n        val_t cosPhiI = cosPhi(v, triI.normal());\n        val_t cosPhiJ = cosPhi(-v, triJ.normal());\n\n        if (cosPhiI <= ZERO || cosPhiJ <= ZERO) continue;\n\n        kij += (cosPhiI * cosPhiJ) / (PI * distSqr);\n    }\n\n    return kij * INV_NUM_RAYS;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Tau (time delay) Computation\n// ============================================================================\n\nint computeTau(const Triangle& triI, const Triangle& triJ) {\n    val_t dist = (triI.center() - triJ.center()).norm();\n    return static_cast<int>(std::ceil(dist * INV_WAVE_SPEED));\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Room Response Simulation State\n// ============================================================================\n\nstruct SimulationState {\n    size_t numTriangles;\n    size_t numTimesteps;\n\n    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\n    std::vector<val_t> areas;       // Area of each triangle\n    std::vector<val_t> rho;         // Reflectivity (0.0 to 1.0)\n    std::vector<val_t> kij;         // Form factors (N x N matrix, row-major)\n    std::vector<int> tau;           // Time delays (N x N matrix, row-major)\n    std::vector<val_t> radE;        // Emission radiosity (T x N matrix)\n    std::vector<val_t> radB;        // Reflected radiosity (T x N matrix)\n    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Computed distances from source\n\n    Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure\n\n    size_t sourceIndex;\n\n    size_t idx2d(size_t i, size_t j) const { return i * numTriangles + j; }\n    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }\n};\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Initialization\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,\n                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {\n    // Generate mesh\n    IcosphereMesh Generation"," // ============================================================================"," ","+// MT19937 engine producing exactly the same output sequence as std::mt19937,","+// but stepping one word at a time so that the state can be jumped ahead in","+// O(polylog) time. This lets every MPI rank start at its exact offset in the","+// single sequential random stream used by the original code.","+class MT19937 {","+public:","+    using result_type = uint32_t;","+    static constexpr int N = 624;","+    static constexpr int M = 397;","+    static constexpr int MEXP = 19937;","+","+    uint32_t x[N];","+    int idx = 0;","+","+    static constexpr result_type min() { return 0; }","+    static constexpr result_type max() { return 0xffffffffu; }","+","+    explicit MT19937(uint32_t seed = 5489u) {","+        x[0] = seed;","+        for (int i = 1; i < N; ++i) {","+            x[i] = 1812433253u * (x[i - 1] ^ (x[i - 1] >> 30)) + static_cast<uint32_t>(i);","+        }","+        idx = 0;","+    }","+","+    // Advance the (linear) state by one word, returning the new raw word","+    inline uint32_t step() {","+        int i1 = idx + 1; i >= 0; --i) {","+            acc.step();","+            if ((g[i >> 6] >> (i & 63)) & 1u) {","+                int a = acc.idx, b = idx;","+                for (int k = 0; k < N; ++k) {","+                    acc.x[a] ^= x[b];","+                    if (++a == N) a = 0;","+                    if (++b == N) b = 0;","+                }","+            }","+        }","+        std::memcpy(x, acc.x, sizeof(x));","+        idx = acc.idx;","+    }","+","+private:","+    using Poly = std::vector<uint64_t>;","+    static constexpr int PW = (MEXP + 63) / 64; k < NS; ++k) {","+                if (g.step() & 1u) {","+                    int pos = NS - 1 - k;","+                    R[pos >> 6] |= uint64_t(1) << (pos & 63);","+                }","+            }","+            auto bits64 = [&](int pos) -> uint64_t {","+                int w = pos >> 6, s = pos & 63;","+                uint64_t v = R[w] >> s;","+                if (s) v |= R[w + 1] << (64 - s);","+                return v;","+            };","+            Poly C(W, 0), B(W, 0), T;","+            C[0] = 1; w >= 0; --w) {","+                    if (!src[w]) continue;","+                    dst[w + ws] ^= src[w] << bs;","+                    if (bs && w + ws + 1 < W) dst[w + ws + 1] ^= src[w] >> (64 - bs);","+                }","+            };","+            for (int n = 0; The cost of a row is dominated by the\n// number of non-culled pairs (each traces NUM_RAYS visibility rays), so rows\n// are split into contiguous blocks of approximately equal cost. Also returns\n// the number of non-culled pairs per row, needed to locate each rank's\n// starting position in the sequential random stream.\nstatic void partitionRows(SimulationState& state, std::vector<int>& pairsPerRow) {\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    const int P = state.nprocs;\n\n    std::vector<int> ub;\n    blockPartition(n, P, ub);\n    std::vector<int>   state.rowStart[r] = static_cast<int>(i);\n    }\n    state.rowBegin = state.rowStart[state.rank];\n    state.rowEnd = state.rowStart[state.rank + 1];\n}\n\nvoid computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {\n    // Tau is computed alongside the form factors for each rank's own rows,\n    // and only stored for the (i, j) pairs that can carry energy (Kij > 0).\n    if (state.rank == 0) rng.skip(drawOffset);\n\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    const size_t numLocal = state.rowEnd - state.rowBegin;\n    state.rowPtr.assign(numLocal + 1, 0);\n    state.colIdx.clear();\n ============================================================================\n\nvoid runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {\n    if (state.rank == 0) printf(\"Running wave propagation simulation...\\n\");\n\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    const int P = state.nprocs;\n    std::vector<int> recvCounts(P), displs(P);\n    for (int r = 0; r < P; ++r) {\n * source radiosity\n                sumB += colWeight[k] * radJ;\n            }\n\n            // Update radiosity: reflection + emission\n            state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] = state.rho[i] * printf(\"  Timestep %zu/%zu\\n\", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {\n    if (state.rank == 0) printf(\"Computing distances via cross-correlation...\\n\");\n\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    const size_t T = state.numTimesteps;\n    const int P = state.nprocs;\n    std::vector<int> part;\n   val_t sum = ZERO;\n\n            for (size_t tt = t; tt < T; ++tt) {\n                sum += srcSeries[tt - t] * series[tt];\n            }\n\n            if (sum > maxCorr) {\n                maxCorr = sum;\n                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);\n            }\n        }\n\n        localDist[i - begin] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);\n    }\n\n    std::vector<int> recvCounts(P), displs(P);\n    for (int r = 0; r < P; ++r) {\n        recvCounts[r] = part[r + 1] - part[r];\n        displs[r] = part[r];\n    }\n    MPI_Gatherv(localDist.data(), ============================================================================\n// Validation\n// ============================================================================\n\nbool validateResults(const SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"\\nValidation:\\n\");\n\n    // Check that distances are non-negative\n    bool allNonNegative = true;\n    val_t minDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    val_t maxDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t sumDist = ZERO;\n    int nonZeroCount = 0;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        val_t d = state.distances[i];\n        if (d < 0) {\n            allNonNegative = false;\n            printf(\"  ERROR: Negative distance at triangle %zu: %f\\n\", i, d);\n        }\n        if (!std::isfinite(d)) {\n            printf(\"  ERROR: Non-finite distance at triangle %zu: %f\\n\", i, d);\n            return false;\n        }\n        minDist = std::min(minDist, d);\n        maxDist = std::max(maxDist, d);\n        sumDist += d;\n        if (d > EPSILON) nonZeroCount++;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Distance range: [%.4f, %.4f]\\n\", minDist, maxDist);\n    printf(\"  Average distance: %.4f\\n\", sumDist / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles));\n    printf(\"  Non-zero distances: %d/%zu\\n\", nonZeroCount, state.numTriangles);\n\n    // Check source distance is zero or very small\n    val_t srcDist = state.distances[state.sourceIndex];\n    if (srcDist > WAVE_SPEED * 2) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Source triangle distance is non-zero: %.4f\\n\", srcDist);\n    }\n\n    // Check radiosity propagation (some triangles should have received energy)\n    int receivedEnergy = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n            if (state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] > EPSILON) {\n                receivedEnergy++;\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Triangles receiving energy: %d/%zu\\n\", receivedEnergy, state.numTriangles);\n\n    if (receivedEnergy == 0) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: No triangles received energy - simulation if (nonZeroKij == 0) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: All form factors are zero - visibility computation failed\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (!allNonNegative) {\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Hash for Verification\n// ============================================================================\n\nuint64_t computeHash(const SimulationState& state) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        const uint32_t* ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint32_t*>(&state.distances[i]);\n        hash ^= (static_cast<uint64_t>(*ptr) + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n    }\n ============================================================================\n\nint getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(int targetTriangles) {\n    // Icosphere: 20 triangles initially, 4x per       subdivisions++;\n        triangles *= 4;\n    }\n    return subdivisions;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Main\n// double simOps = static_cast<double>(n * n * t);\n    double distOps = static_cast<double>(n * t * t);\n\n    printf(\"\\nPerformance:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Triangles: %zu\\n\", n);\n    printf(\"   // Convert distances to double for output\n        std::vector<double> distData(state.distances.begin(), state.distances.end());\n        print_results(distData, \"Distances\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        if (!validateResults(state)) {\n            fflush(stdout);\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return       // Area of each triangle\n    std::vector<val_t> rho;         // Reflectivity (0.0 to 1.0)\n    std::vector<val_t> radE;        // Emission radiosity (T x N matrix)\n    std::vector<val_t> radB;        // Reflected radiosity (T x N matrix, replicated)\n    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Computed distances from source (gathered on rank 0)\n\n    // MPI decomposition: each rank owns a contiguous block of receiver rows i.\n    int rank = 0;\n    int nprocs = 1;\n    std::vector<int> rowStart;      // nprocs + 1 entries\n    size_t rowBegin = 0, rowEnd = 0;\n\n    // Local rows of the form factor / time delay matrices, stored sparsely\n    // (only entries with Kij > 0, which are the only ones that contribute).\n    std::vector<size_t> rowPtr;     // (rowEnd - rowBegin) + 1 entries\n    std::vector<idx_t> colIdx;      // emitter triangle j\n    std::vector<int> colTau;        // Tau(i, j)\n    std::vector<val_t> colWeight;   // min(Kij * area_j, 1)\n    long long nonZeroKij = 0;       // global count of Kij > EPSILON\n\n    Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure\n\n    size_t sourceIndex;\n\n    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }\n};\n\n// Uniform block partition of n items over p parts\nstatic void blockPartition(size_t n, int p, std::vector<int>& start) {\n    start.resize(p + 1);\n    for (int r = 0; r <= p; ++r) start[r] = static_cast<int>((n * static_cast<size_t>(r)) / p);\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Initialization\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,\n                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {\n    const bool root = state.rank == 0;\n\n    // Generate mesh (replicated on every rank; cheap compared to Kij)\n    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);  // Radius 10 units\n    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);\n    state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();\n    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;\n    state.sourceIndex = sourceIdx % state.numTriangles;\n\n    if (root) printf(\"Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\\n\", state.numTriangles);\n\n    // Build octree for spatial acceleration\n    if (root) printf(\"Building octree...\\n\");\n    state.octree.build(state.triangles);\n\n    // Initialize areas\n    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();\n    }\n\n    // Initialize reflectivity\n    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);\n\n    // Initialize matrices\n    state.radE.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.radB.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.distances.resize(state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n\n    // Set source emission (active for first half of timesteps)\n    size_t timeOn = 0;\n    size_t timeOff = timesteps / 2;\n    for (size_t t = timeOn; t < timeOff; ++t) {\n        state.radE[state.idxTN(t, state.sourceIndex)] = 1.0f;\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Precomputation Phase\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Number of random draws consumed by computeKij for a non-culled pair\nconstexpr uint64_t DRAWS_PER_PAIR = 4 * NUM_RAYS;\n\nstatic inline bool pairCulled(const Triangle& a, const Triangle& b) {\n    return a.normal().dot(b.normal()) > 0.99f;\n}\n\n// Decide the row decomposition. The cost of a row is dominated by the\n// number of non-culled pairs (each traces NUM_RAYS visibility rays), so rows\n// are split into contiguous blocks of approximately equal cost. Also returns\n// the number of non-culled pairs per row, needed to locate each rank's\n// starting position in the sequential random stream.\nstatic void partitionRows(SimulationState& state, std::vector<int>& pairsPerRow) {\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    const int P = state.nprocs;\n\n    std::vector<int> ub;\n    blockPartition(n, P, ub);\n    std::vector<int> localCounts(ub[state.rank + 1] - ub[state.rank]);\n    for (int i = ub[state.rank]; i < ub[state.rank + 1]; ++i) {\n        int cnt = 0;\n        const Triangle& ti = state.triangles[i];\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            if (static_cast<size_t>(i) == j) continue;\n            if (!pairCulled(ti, state.triangles[j])) ++cnt;\n        }\n        localCounts[i - ub[state.rank]] = cnt;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<int> recvCounts(P), displs(P);\n    for (int r = 0; r < P; ++r) {\n        recvCounts[r] = ub[r + 1] - ub[r];\n        displs[r] = ub[r];\n    }\n    pairsPerRow.assign(n, 0);\n    MPI_Allgatherv(localCounts.data(), static_cast<int>(localCounts.size()), MPI_INT,\n                   pairsPerRow.data(), recvCounts.data(), displs.data(), MPI_INT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    // Cost model: rays dominate; add a small per-row term for the j loop\n    std::vector<double> prefix(n + 1, 0.0);\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        prefix[i + 1] = prefix[i] + static_cast<double>(pairsPerRow[i]) * NUM_RAYS +\n                        0.05 * static_cast<double>(n);\n    }\n    const double total = prefix[n];\n    state.rowStart.assign(P + 1, 0);\n    state.rowStart[P] = static_cast<int>(n);\n    size_t i = 0;\n    for (int r = 1; r < P; ++r) {\n        double target = total * r / P;\n        while (i < n && prefix[i] < target) ++i;\n        state.rowStart[r] = static_cast<int>(i);\n    }\n    state.rowBegin = state.rowStart[state.rank];\n    state.rowEnd = state.rowStart[state.rank + 1];\n}\n\nvoid computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {\n    // Tau is computed alongside the form factors for each rank's own rows,\n    // and only stored for the (i, j) pairs that can carry energy (Kij > 0).\n    if (state.rank == 0) printf(\"Computing time delays (Tau)...\\n\");\n}\n\nvoid computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {\n    if (state.rank == 0) printf(\"Computing form factors (Kij)...\\n\");\n\n    std::vector<int> pairsPerRow;\n    partitionRows(state, pairsPerRow);\n\n    // Position this rank's generator exactly where the sequential code's\n    // generator would be when starting row rowBegin.\n    uint64_t drawOffset = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.rowBegin; ++i) {\n        drawOffset += static_cast<uint64_t>(pairsPerRow[i]) * DRAWS_PER_PAIR;\n    }\n    RandomGenerator rng(42);\n    rng.skip(drawOffset);\n\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    const size_t numLocal = state.rowEnd - state.rowBegin;\n    state.rowPtr.assign(numLocal + 1, 0);\n    state.colIdx.clear();\n    state.colTau.clear();\n    state.colWeight.clear();\n    long long localNonZero = 0;\n\n    for (size_t i = state.rowBegin; i < state.rowEnd; ++i) {\n        const Triangle& triI = state.triangles[i];\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            if (i == j) continue;\n            val_t kij = computeKij(i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);\n            if (kij > EPSILON) ++localNonZero;\n            if (kij > ZERO) {\n                state.colIdx.push_back(static_cast<idx_t>(j));\n                state.colTau.push_back(computeTau(triI, state.triangles[j]));\n                state.colWeight.push_back(std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE));\n            }\n        }\n        state.rowPtr[i - state.rowBegin + 1] = state.colIdx.size();\n    }\n\n    MPI_Allreduce(&localNonZero, &state.nonZeroKij, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG, MPI_SUM, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    if (state.rank == 0) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n            if ((i + 1) % 100 == 0 || i + 1 == n) {\n                printf(\"  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\\n\", i + 1, n);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {\n    if (state.rank == 0) printf(\"Running wave propagation simulation...\\n\");\n\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    const int P = state.nprocs;\n    std::vector<int> recvCounts(P), displs(P);\n    for (int r = 0; r < P; ++r) {\n        recvCounts[r] = state.rowStart[r + 1] - state.rowStart[r];\n        displs[r] = state.rowStart[r];\n    }\n\n    const idx_t* colIdx = state.colIdx.data();\n    const int* colTau = state.colTau.data();\n    const val_t* colWeight = state.colWeight.data();\n    const val_t* radB = state.radB.data();\n\n    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n        const int ti = static_cast<int>(t);\n        for (size_t i = state.rowBegin; i < state.rowEnd; ++i) {\n            val_t sumB = ZERO;\n\n            const size_t kBeg = state.rowPtr[i - state.rowBegin];\n            const size_t kEnd = state.rowPtr[i - state.rowBegin + 1];\n            for (size_t k = kBeg; k < kEnd; ++k) {\n                int tauij = colTau[k];\n\n                // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i\n                if (ti < tauij) continue;\n\n                // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred\n                size_t srcTime = t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);\n                val_t radJ = radB[srcTime * n + colIdx[k]];\n                if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;\n\n                // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity\n                sumB += colWeight[k] * radJ;\n            }\n\n            // Update radiosity: reflection + emission\n            state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] = state.rho[i] * sumB + state.radE[state.idxTN(t, i)];\n        }\n\n        // Share this timestep's radiosities with all ranks\n        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DATATYPE_NULL, &state.radB[state.idxTN(t, 0)],\n                       recvCounts.data(), displs.data(), MPI_FLOAT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        if (state.rank == 0 && ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps)) {\n            printf(\"  Timestep %zu/%zu\\n\", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {\n    if (state.rank == 0) printf(\"Computing distances via cross-correlation...\\n\");\n\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    const size_t T = state.numTimesteps;\n    const int P = state.nprocs;\n    std::vector<int> part;\n    blockPartition(n, P, part);\n    const size_t begin = part[state.rank], end = part[state.rank + 1];\n\n    std::vector<val_t> srcSeries(T), series(T);\n    for (size_t t = 0; t < T; ++t) srcSeries[t] = state.radB[state.idxTN(t, state.sourceIndex)];\n\n    std::vector<val_t> localDist(end - begin);\n    for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < T; ++t) series[t] = state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)];\n\n        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;\n        int bestT = 0;\n\n        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < T; ++t) {\n            val_t sum = ZERO;\n\n            for (size_t tt = t; tt < T; ++tt) {\n                sum += srcSeries[tt - t] * series[tt];\n            }\n\n            if (sum > maxCorr) {\n                maxCorr = sum;\n                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);\n            }\n        }\n\n        localDist[i - begin] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);\n    }\n\n    std::vector<int> recvCounts(P), displs(P);\n    for (int r = 0; r < P; ++r) {\n        recvCounts[r] = part[r + 1] - part[r];\n        displs[r] = part[r];\n    }\n    MPI_Gatherv(localDist.data(), static_cast<int>(localDist.size()), MPI_FLOAT,\n                state.distances.data(), recvCounts.data(), displs.data(), MPI_FLOAT,\n                0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Validation\n// ============================================================================\n\nbool validateResults(const SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"\\nValidation:\\n\");\n\n    // Check that distances are non-negative\n    bool allNonNegative = true;\n    val_t minDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    val_t maxDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t sumDist = ZERO;\n    int nonZeroCount = 0;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        val_t d = state.distances[i];\n        if (d < 0) {\n            allNonNegative = false;\n            printf(\"  ERROR: Negative distance at triangle %zu: %f\\n\", i, d);\n        }\n        if (!std::isfinite(d)) {\n            printf(\"  ERROR: Non-finite distance at triangle %zu: %f\\n\", i, d);\n            return false;\n        }\n        minDist = std::min(minDist, d);\n        maxDist = std::max(maxDist, d);\n        sumDist += d;\n        if (d > EPSILON) nonZeroCount++;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Distance range: [%.4f, %.4f]\\n\", minDist, maxDist);\n    printf(\"  Average distance: %.4f\\n\", sumDist / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles));\n    printf(\"  Non-zero distances: %d/%zu\\n\", nonZeroCount, state.numTriangles);\n\n    // Check source distance is zero or very small\n    val_t srcDist = state.distances[state.sourceIndex];\n    if (srcDist > WAVE_SPEED * 2) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Source triangle distance is non-zero: %.4f\\n\", srcDist);\n    }\n\n    // Check radiosity propagation (some triangles should have received energy)\n    int receivedEnergy = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n            if (state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] > EPSILON) {\n                receivedEnergy++;\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Triangles receiving energy: %d/%zu\\n\", receivedEnergy, state.numTriangles);\n\n    if (receivedEnergy == 0) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: No triangles received energy - simulation failed\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check Kij matrix (should have some non-zero entries)\n    int nonZeroKij = static_cast<int>(state.nonZeroKij);\n    printf(\"  Non-zero form factors: %d/%zu (%.2f%%)\\n\",\n           nonZeroKij, state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles,\n           100.0f * nonZeroKij / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles));\n\n    if (nonZeroKij == 0) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: All form factors are zero - visibility computation failed\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (!allNonNegative) {\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Hash for Verification\n// ============================================================================\n\nuint64_t computeHash(const SimulationState& state) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        const uint32_t* ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint32_t*>(&state.distances[i]);\n        hash ^= (static_cast<uint64_t>(*ptr) + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Helper: Compute subdivision level from target triangle count\n// ============================================================================\n\nint getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(int targetTriangles) {\n    // Icosphere: 20 triangles initially, 4x per subdivision\n    // subdivisions: 0->20, 1->80, 2->320, 3->1280, 4->5120, 5->20480\n    int subdivisions = 0;\n    int triangles = 20;\n    while (triangles < targetTriangles && subdivisions < 6) {\n        subdivisions++;\n        triangles *= 4;\n    }\n    return subdivisions;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Main\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Target number of triangles (default: 320)\\n\");\n    printf(\"               Actual count will be rounded to nearest icosphere level:\\n\");\n    printf(\"               20, 80, 320, 1280, 5120, 20480\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <num>     Number of timesteps (default: 50)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Source triangle index (default: 0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r <val>     Reflectivity 0.0-1.0 (default: 0.8)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -o           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nstatic int finish(int code) {\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return code;\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n    int rank = 0, nprocs = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nprocs);\n    const bool root = rank == 0;\n\n    int targetTriangles = 320;\n    int timesteps = 50;\n    int sourceIdx = 0;\n    val_t reflectivity = 0.8f;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            timesteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sourceIdx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            reflectivity = static_cast<val_t>(atof(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-o\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (root) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return        }\n    }\n\n    int subdivisions = getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(targetTriangles);\n\n    if (root) {\n        printf(\"Room Response Simulation Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"===================================\\n\");\n        printf(\"Target triangles: %d (using %d subdivisions)\\n\", targetTriangles, subdivisions);\n        printf(\"Timesteps: %d\\n\",    if (root) {\n        printf(\"Precomputation time: %ld ms\\n\", preDuration);\n        printf(\"\\n\");\n    }\n\n    // Simulation\n    auto startSim = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto distDuration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(endDist - startDist).count();\n\n    if (!root) return finish(0);\n\n double simOps = static_cast<double>(n * n * t);\n    double distOps = static_cast<double>(n * t * t);\n\n    printf(\"\\nPerformance:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Triangles: %zu\\n\", n);\n    printf(\"   // Convert distances to double for output\n        std::vector<double> distData(state.distances.begin(), state.distances.end());\n        print_results(distData, \"Distances\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        if (!validateResults(state)) {\n            fflush(stdout);\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return       // Area of each triangle\n    std::vector<val_t> rho;         // Reflectivity (0.0 to 1.0)\n    std::vector<val_t> radE;        // Emission radiosity (T x N matrix)\n    std::vector<val_t> radB;        // Reflected radiosity (T x N matrix, replicated)\n    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Computed distances from source (gathered on rank 0)\n\n    // MPI decomposition: each rank owns a contiguous block of receiver rows i.\n    int rank = 0;\n    int nprocs = 1;\n    std::vector<int> rowStart;      // nprocs + 1 entries\n    size_t rowBegin = 0, rowEnd = 0;\n\n    // Local rows of the form factor / time delay matrices, stored sparsely\n    // (only entries with Kij > 0, which are the only ones that contribute).\n    std::vector<size_t> rowPtr;     // (rowEnd - rowBegin) + 1 entries\n    std::vector<idx_t> colIdx;      // emitter triangle j\n    std::vector<int> colTau;        // Tau(i, j)\n    std::vector<val_t> colWeight;   // min(Kij * area_j, 1)\n    long long nonZeroKij = 0;       // global count of Kij > EPSILON\n\n    Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure\n\n    size_t sourceIndex;\n\n    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }\n};\n\n// Uniform block partition of n items over p parts\nstatic void blockPartition(size_t n, int p, std::vector<int>& start) {\n    start.resize(p + 1);\n    for (int r = 0; r <= p; ++r) start[r] = static_cast<int>((n * static_cast<size_t>(r)) / p);\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Initialization\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,\n                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {\n    const bool root = state.rank == 0;\n\n    // Generate mesh (replicated on every rank; cheap compared to Kij)\n    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);  // Radius 10 units\n    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);\n    state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();\n    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;\n    state.sourceIndex = sourceIdx % state.numTriangles;\n\n    if (root) printf(\"Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\\n\", state.numTriangles);\n\n    // Build octree for spatial acceleration\n    if (root) printf(\"Building octree...\\n\");\n    state.octree.build(state.triangles);\n\n    // Initialize areas\n    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();\n    }\n\n    // Initialize reflectivity\n    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);\n\n    // Initialize matrices\n    state.radE.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.radB.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.distances.resize(state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n\n    // Set source emission (active for first half of timesteps)\n    size_t timeOn = 0;\n    size_t timeOff = timesteps / 2;\n    for (size_t t = timeOn; t < timeOff; ++t) {\n        state.radE[state.idxTN(t, state.sourceIndex)] = 1.0f;\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Precomputation Phase\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Number of random draws consumed by computeKij for a non-culled pair\nconstexpr uint64_t DRAWS_PER_PAIR = 4 * NUM_RAYS;\n\nstatic inline bool pairCulled(const Triangle& a, const Triangle& b) {\n    return a.normal().dot(b.normal()) > 0.99f;\n}\n\n// Decide the row decomposition. The cost of a row is dominated by the\n// number of non-culled pairs (each traces NUM_RAYS visibility rays), so rows\n// are split into contiguous blocks of approximately equal cost. Also returns\n// the number of non-culled pairs per row, needed to locate each rank's\n// starting position in the sequential random stream.\nstatic void partitionRows(SimulationState& state, std::vector<int>& pairsPerRow) {\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    const int P = state.nprocs;\n\n    std::vector<int> ub;\n    blockPartition(n, P, ub);\n    std::vector<int> localCounts(ub[state.rank + 1] - ub[state.rank]);\n    for (int i = ub[state.rank]; i < ub[state.rank + 1]; ++i) {\n        int cnt = 0;\n        const Triangle& ti = state.triangles[i];\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            if (static_cast<size_t>(i) == j) continue;\n            if (!pairCulled(ti, state.triangles[j])) ++cnt;\n        }\n        localCounts[i - ub[state.rank]] = cnt;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<int> recvCounts(P), displs(P);\n    for (int r = 0; r < P; ++r) {\n        recvCounts[r] = ub[r + 1] - ub[r];\n        displs[r] = ub[r];\n    }\n    pairsPerRow.assign(n, 0);\n    MPI_Allgatherv(localCounts.data(), static_cast<int>(localCounts.size()), MPI_INT,\n                   pairsPerRow.data(), recvCounts.data(), displs.data(), MPI_INT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    // Cost model: rays dominate; add a small per-row term for the j loop\n    std::vector<double> prefix(n + 1, 0.0);\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        prefix[i + 1] = prefix[i] + static_cast<double>(pairsPerRow[i]) * NUM_RAYS +\n                        0.05 * static_cast<double>(n);\n    }\n    const double total = prefix[n];\n    state.rowStart.assign(P + 1, 0);\n    state.rowStart[P] = static_cast<int>(n);\n    size_t i = 0;\n    for (int r = 1; r < P; ++r) {\n        double target = total * r / P;\n        while (i < n && prefix[i] < target) ++i;\n        state.rowStart[r] = static_cast<int>(i);\n    }\n    state.rowBegin = state.rowStart[state.rank];\n    state.rowEnd = state.rowStart[state.rank + 1];\n}\n\nvoid computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {\n    // Tau is computed alongside the form factors for each rank's own rows,\n    // and only stored for the (i, j) pairs that can carry energy (Kij > 0).\n    if (state.rank == 0) printf(\"Computing time delays (Tau)...\\n\");\n}\n\nvoid computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {\n    if (state.rank == 0) printf(\"Computing form factors (Kij)...\\n\");\n\n    std::vector<int> pairsPerRow;\n    partitionRows(state, pairsPerRow);\n\n    // Position this rank's generator exactly where the sequential code's\n    // generator would be when starting row rowBegin.\n    uint64_t drawOffset = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.rowBegin; ++i) {\n        drawOffset += static_cast<uint64_t>(pairsPerRow[i]) * DRAWS_PER_PAIR;\n    }\n    RandomGenerator rng(42);\n    rng.skip(drawOffset);\n\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    const size_t numLocal = state.rowEnd - state.rowBegin;\n    state.rowPtr.assign(numLocal + 1, 0);\n    state.colIdx.clear();\n    state.colTau.clear();\n    state.colWeight.clear();\n    long long localNonZero = 0;\n\n    for (size_t i = state.rowBegin; i < state.rowEnd; ++i) {\n        const Triangle& triI = state.triangles[i];\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            if (i == j) continue;\n            val_t kij = computeKij(i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);\n            if (kij > EPSILON) ++localNonZero;\n            if (kij > ZERO) {\n                state.colIdx.push_back(static_cast<idx_t>(j));\n                state.colTau.push_back(computeTau(triI, state.triangles[j]));\n                state.colWeight.push_back(std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE));\n            }\n        }\n        state.rowPtr[i - state.rowBegin + 1] = state.colIdx.size();\n    }\n\n    MPI_Allreduce(&localNonZero, &state.nonZeroKij, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG, MPI_SUM, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    if (state.rank == 0) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n            if ((i + 1) % 100 == 0 || i + 1 == n) {\n                printf(\"  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\\n\", i + 1, n);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {\n    if (state.rank == 0) printf(\"Running wave propagation simulation...\\n\");\n\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    const int P = state.nprocs;\n    std::vector<int> recvCounts(P), displs(P);\n    for (int r = 0; r < P; ++r) {\n        recvCounts[r] = state.rowStart[r + 1] - state.rowStart[r];\n        displs[r] = state.rowStart[r];\n    }\n\n    const idx_t* colIdx = state.colIdx.data();\n    const int* colTau = state.colTau.data();\n    const val_t* colWeight = state.colWeight.data();\n    const val_t* radB = state.radB.data();\n\n    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n        const int ti = static_cast<int>(t);\n        for (size_t i = state.rowBegin; i < state.rowEnd; ++i) {\n            val_t sumB = ZERO;\n\n            const size_t kBeg = state.rowPtr[i - state.rowBegin];\n            const size_t kEnd = state.rowPtr[i - state.rowBegin + 1];\n            for (size_t k = kBeg; k < kEnd; ++k) {\n                int tauij = colTau[k];\n\n                // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i\n                if (ti < tauij) continue;\n\n                // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred\n                size_t srcTime = t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);\n                val_t radJ = radB[srcTime * n + colIdx[k]];\n                if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;\n\n                // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity\n                sumB += colWeight[k] * radJ;\n            }\n\n            // Update radiosity: reflection + emission\n            state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] = state.rho[i] * sumB + state.radE[state.idxTN(t, i)];\n        }\n\n        // Share this timestep's radiosities with all ranks\n        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DATATYPE_NULL, &state.radB[state.idxTN(t, 0)],\n                       recvCounts.data(), displs.data(), MPI_FLOAT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        if (state.rank == 0 && ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps)) {\n            printf(\"  Timestep %zu/%zu\\n\", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {\n    if (state.rank == 0) printf(\"Computing distances via cross-correlation...\\n\");\n\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    const size_t T = state.numTimesteps;\n    const int P = state.nprocs;\n    std::vector<int> part;\n    blockPartition(n, P, part);\n    const size_t begin = part[state.rank], end = part[state.rank + 1];\n\n    std::vector<val_t> srcSeries(T), series(T);\n    for (size_t t = 0; t < T; ++t) srcSeries[t] = state.radB[state.idxTN(t, state.sourceIndex)];\n\n    std::vector<val_t> localDist(end - begin);\n    for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < T; ++t) series[t] = state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)];\n\n        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;\n        int bestT = 0;\n\n        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < T; ++t) {\n            val_t sum = ZERO;\n\n            for (size_t tt = t; tt < T; ++tt) {\n                sum += srcSeries[tt - t] * series[tt];\n            }\n\n            if (sum > maxCorr) {\n                maxCorr = sum;\n                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);\n            }\n        }\n\n        localDist[i - begin] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);\n    }\n\n    std::vector<int> recvCounts(P), displs(P);\n    for (int r = 0; r < P; ++r) {\n        recvCounts[r] = part[r + 1] - part[r];\n        displs[r] = part[r];\n    }\n    MPI_Gatherv(localDist.data(), static_cast<int>(localDist.size()), MPI_FLOAT,\n                state.distances.data(), recvCounts.data(), displs.data(), MPI_FLOAT,\n                0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Validation\n// ============================================================================\n\nbool validateResults(const SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"\\nValidation:\\n\");\n\n    // Check that distances are non-negative\n    bool allNonNegative = true;\n    val_t minDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    val_t maxDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t sumDist = ZERO;\n    int nonZeroCount = 0;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        val_t d = state.distances[i];\n        if (d < 0) {\n            allNonNegative = false;\n            printf(\"  ERROR: Negative distance at triangle %zu: %f\\n\", i, d);\n        }\n        if (!std::isfinite(d)) {\n            printf(\"  ERROR: Non-finite distance at triangle %zu: %f\\n\", i, d);\n            return false;\n        }\n        minDist = std::min(minDist, d);\n        maxDist = std::max(maxDist, d);\n        sumDist += d;\n        if (d > EPSILON) nonZeroCount++;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Distance range: [%.4f, %.4f]\\n\", minDist, maxDist);\n    printf(\"  Average distance: %.4f\\n\", sumDist / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles));\n    printf(\"  Non-zero distances: %d/%zu\\n\", nonZeroCount, state.numTriangles);\n\n    // Check source distance is zero or very small\n    val_t srcDist = state.distances[state.sourceIndex];\n    if (srcDist > WAVE_SPEED * 2) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Source triangle distance is non-zero: %.4f\\n\", srcDist);\n    }\n\n    // Check radiosity propagation (some triangles should have received energy)\n    int receivedEnergy = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n            if (state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] > EPSILON) {\n                receivedEnergy++;\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Triangles receiving energy: %d/%zu\\n\", receivedEnergy, state.numTriangles);\n\n    if (receivedEnergy == 0) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: No triangles received energy - simulation failed\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check Kij matrix (should have some non-zero entries)\n    int nonZeroKij = static_cast<int>(state.nonZeroKij);\n    printf(\"  Non-zero form factors: %d/%zu (%.2f%%)\\n\",\n           nonZeroKij, state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles,\n           100.0f * nonZeroKij / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles));\n\n    if (nonZeroKij == 0) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: All form factors are zero - visibility computation failed\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (!allNonNegative) {\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Hash for Verification\n// ============================================================================\n\nuint64_t computeHash(const SimulationState& state) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        const uint32_t* ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint32_t*>(&state.distances[i]);\n        hash ^= (static_cast<uint64_t>(*ptr) + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Helper: Compute subdivision level from target triangle count\n// ============================================================================\n\nint getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(int targetTriangles) {\n    // Icosphere: 20 triangles initially, 4x per subdivision\n    // subdivisions: 0->20, 1->80, 2->320, 3->1280, 4->5120, 5->20480\n    int subdivisions = 0;\n    int triangles = 20;\n    while (triangles < targetTriangles && subdivisions < 6) {\n        subdivisions++;\n        triangles *= 4;\n    }\n    return subdivisions;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Main\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Target number of triangles (default: 320)\\n\");\n    printf(\"               Actual count will be rounded to nearest icosphere level:\\n\");\n    printf(\"               20, 80, 320, 1280, 5120, 20480\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <num>     Number of timesteps (default: 50)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Source triangle index (default: 0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r <val>     Reflectivity 0.0-1.0 (default: 0.8)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -o           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nstatic int finish(int code) {\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return code;\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n    int rank = 0, nprocs = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nprocs);\n    const bool root = rank == 0;\n\n    int targetTriangles = 320;\n    int timesteps = 50;\n    int sourceIdx = 0;\n    val_t reflectivity = 0.8f;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            timesteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sourceIdx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            reflectivity = static_cast<val_t>(atof(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-o\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (root) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return printf(\"===================================\\n\");\n        printf(\"Target triangles: %d (using %d subdivisions)\\n\", targetTriangles, subdivisions);\n        printf(\"Timesteps: %d\\n\", MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto endPre = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto preDuration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(endPre - startPre).count();\n\n    if (root) {\n        printf(\"Precomputation time: %ld ms\\n\", preDuration);\n        printf(\"\\n\");\n    }\n\n    // Simulation\n    auto startSim = I replaced `std::mt19937` with an equivalent generator (I tested it against the standard one) that can skip ahead to any position in about 30 ms. Each rank first counts how many draws come before its rows, then jumps straight there. So every rank uses exactly the random numbers the serial code would have used for those rows.\n- **Splitting the work:** each rank gets a contiguous block of rows, sized so the amount of ray-tracing is roughly equal. Each rank stores only its own rows, keeping just the non-zero form factors with their time delays and weights. The full N×N matrices are never built or sent. The non-zero count used by validation is summed across ranks.\n- **Simulation:** each rank updates its own rows, then all ranks share one timestep's values. This works because every time delay is at least one step. Each row is still summed in the original order, so the floating-point results are identical.\n- **Distances:** the rows are split evenly across ranks and the results are collected on rank 0.\n- **Output:** only rank 0 prints. Timers stop only after all ranks finish. The form-factor progress lines are printed together after that phase rather than as it runs. There is one new header line, `MPI ranks: N`.
